## Chinese Independent Developer Projects List
> 🌐 **[🇬🇧 English README](README.md) | [🇨🇳 中文 README](README-zh.md)**

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Notice**: This is the English version of the README maintained on this fork for personal tracking and reference. Upstream updates from the original repository ([1c7/chinese-independent-developer](https://github.com/1c7/chinese-independent-developer)) are synced periodically.

Aggregate all projects from Chinese independent developers

### Sub-boards
- [Programmer Board](./pages/README-Programmer-Edition.md): Requires command line or coding to use
- [Game Board](./pages/README-Game.md): All are games
- [2018 ~ 2020 Project List](./pages/README-2018-2020.md): Archived separately due to GitHub rendering limits

Note: You are currently viewing the main board. The products collected here are ready-to-use out of the box, which is different from the types of products in the sub-boards.

**1. Why does this list exist?**
As developers, we are actually quite curious about what side projects other people are working on (whether the goal is to be profitable / play around / give it a try)
So we specially created this repository. All developers are welcome to add your own projects~ Just submit a Pull Request or Issue <br/>
(Selection criteria: Must be a website or App, cannot be a developer tool or forum-type website)

**Writing a good introduction is important**: ❌ `AI video generation tool` → ✅ `Generate vertical short videos in 3 steps without video editing experience`<br/>
👉 [How to write a good one-sentence product introduction](./CONTRIBUTING.md)

**2. Projects have 3 states**

| In Development | Live | Closed or Lacking Maintenance |
|--------|--------|--------|
| :clock8: | :white_check_mark: | :x: |

## Recommend some helpful websites for independent developers:

* [Failory](https://www.failory.com/) - Share stories of entrepreneurial failure
* [Indie Hacker](https://www.indiehackers.com/)
* [Failory](https://www.failory.com/) - Share stories of entrepreneurial failure
* [Starter Story](https://www.starterstory.com/) - Interview with e-commerce (e-commerce) profit story, very similar to Indie hacker, but focused on e-commerce field
* [Awesome Indie](https://github.com/mezod/awesome-indie)
* [MicroConf Videos](http://www.microconf.com/starter/past-videos/)
* [Stripe Atlas Guide](https://stripe.com/atlas/guides)
* [Opps Daily - This newsletter is great, I recommend subscribing](https://www.oppslist.com/)
* [Master of Scale](https://mastersofscale.com)
* [NomadList](https://nomadlist.com/) - You can use NomadList to choose which city to go to when working remotely

* [v2ex Forum - Sharing and Creation Section](https://www.v2ex.com/go/create)

#### sitecheck - [Github](https://github.com/mymickey/sitecheck)
* :white_check_mark: [SiteCheck](https://sitecheck.top): SiteCheck is a MacOS desktop software inspired by https://ipcheck.ing. The official website is sitecheck.top. I have always been a loyal user of ipcheck.ing. Usually, when switching proxies, troubleshooting lines, or checking DNS exits, my first reaction is to open it and take a look. Because I've used it for long enough, I gradually had the idea of making a similar small tool myself. SiteCheck is built on this basis, a lighter local app specifically resident in the macOS menubar, pulling out the few things I use most often: checking connectivity, checking DNS tests, and conveniently checking the current public IP. It is not intended to replace ipcheck.ing, but to turn the experience that I really repeatedly click every day into a more direct small tool. Also, a tribute to Achan for bringing us such an easy-to-use tool.
